# UI slice S1 record: market band on every screen + Today's first fold

Branch `claude/ui-abc-s1`, from main `01066ebe`. One section per package; it grows with each package. Every number is tagged
**実測** (measured here, with its command) or **未検証** (not measured). "The harness" is the round-3 measurement harness and
"the yardstick" the S1-P0 tools (state matrix, values, screenshots, ledger, phrases, surfaces, timing); both are scratch tools
outside the repository, never committed. bd is not installed on this machine, so each package is tracked here instead.

## S1-P1 Lazy stock seam (pure refactor; nothing visible changes)

Status: done. Code: `ebcea738` (variant A kept; see "Variant choice at 1440"); `6495f969` fixes one precondition of the new
spec (see "Deliberate test changes"); the record commits change docs only.

### What changed

- `frontend/src/static/brief/stockViewLoader.js` (new): the one `import('./StockView')`, with a loaded/failed store like
  `priceChartLoader.js` (`loadedStockView`, `loadStockView`, `stockViewFailed`, `subscribeStockView`). A failed load is retried
  on the next call.
- `frontend/src/static/brief/StockScreen.jsx`: `StockView` is `lazy(...)` under `Suspense`.
  - A module already loaded is handed to `React.lazy` as a thenable that resolves at once, so the screen renders in the same
    pass, with no fallback frame (a plain `lazy(() => import(...))` suspends once even when the chunk is loaded).
  - The fallback is the screen's own loading line 「銘柄データを読み込み中…」 (the same text `StockView` shows before its
    data; no new string), under the desktop header, so the pane keeps its header and place.
  - A failed load resolves to the failure box instead of throwing (see "Deviation" below): 「銘柄の画面を読み込めません
    （通信状態を確認して、再読み込みしてください）」 and a 44 px 再読み込み button that reloads the page, as the chart box's
    does (`PriceChart.jsx`). The desktop header stays above it. If a later load succeeds, the box gives way to the screen.
  - A render error inside `StockView` is not caught: it propagates exactly as on main.
  - The failure text mirrors the chart box's (including its 「再読み込みしてください」). It appears in no yardstick view (no
    view blocks a chunk), so the phrase scan does not see it; it is recovery advice, not a buy recommendation.
- `frontend/src/static/brief/BriefApp.jsx`:
  - imports `./priceChartLoader` (its `loadPriceChart`), so the chart renderer's idle-time prefetch stays in the shell;
  - prefetches the stock chunk in the same idle callback as the market tab's chunk;
  - starts the stock chunk at module start where a stock is on the first screen: at >= 1024 px (the stock pane; this is
    variant A of the order, kept by measurement) and on a stock's own address (`#/brief/stock/…`);
  - on a phone, `open()` navigates at once when the chunk is loaded, and otherwise when `loadStockView()` settles (resolved or
    rejected): the forward view transition never captures a loading screen, and a failed load still reaches the failure box.
    The scroll position is remembered at the tap; only the last tap opens, and none if the screen changed meanwhile; the chart
    renderer's chunk is requested alongside. At >= 1024 px `open()` never waits. (S1-P1b bounds the phone's wait at 400 ms:
    after it the stock opens on its loading line.)
- `frontend/src/static/brief/brief-chart.css`: `.brief-stock-failed` (the chart box's type and button, 44 px at every width).
  The chart box's own rules are untouched.

Design Acceptance (`frontend/tools/design-review.mjs`, 0 edits): the chunk is named `StockView-<hash>.js`, which no Design
Acceptance pattern matches (`/\/priceChart[-.][^/]*\.js/` keeps matching the renderer chunk alone); every selector it reads
(`brief-candidate`, `brief-conclusion`, `.brief-stockhead h2 span:first-child`, `.brief-bar-title b span`,
`.brief-chart-failed`, the candles) is rendered as before. The failure box has its own class, so it is never read as the chart
chunk's failure. The release proof's source markers (`className="brief-stockhead"` in `StockScreen.jsx`, the stock route in
`BriefApp.jsx`) are unchanged.

### Deviation from the order

The order names "a small error boundary". The failure box is reached without one: the lazy component's load resolves a failed
import to the box. Reasons:
- 実測: with a boundary in place of the resolved failure (a scratch edit of `StockScreen.jsx`, reverted, run with the phone
  case of `brief-lazy.spec.js` on the dev server), the box rendered but the page reported the caught import failure twice as
  uncaught page errors (「Failed to fetch dynamically imported module: …/StockView.jsx」), which the static specs count
  (`expect(errors).toEqual([])`). React's development build replays a failed render outside the boundary.
- A boundary would also catch real render errors of the screen, which main lets propagate.

The behaviour asked for (a failure box with a 44 px 再読み込み that reloads the page) is the same.

### Deliberate test changes

None to existing tests. `StockView.test.jsx` and the card tests import `StockView` directly, and no vitest file renders
`StockScreen` or `BriefApp`'s stock route (on main, `grep -rln "StockScreen\|BriefApp" frontend/src --include='*.test.*'`
lists only `briefNav.test.js` and `BriefParts.test.jsx`, which name them in comments; `App.static.test.jsx` opens an old
symbol link and checks only the hash it lands on). The precache and build tool tests enumerate no
real chunk: `precacheAssets` follows every dynamic import, so the new chunk is precached with no change.

New tests:
- `frontend/src/static/brief/StockScreen.test.jsx` (6): a loaded chunk renders in the first pass with no loading frame; the
  loading line under the desktop header while the chunk loads; the failure box and its reload; after a failed load the screen
  opens on the box at once and draws once a later load succeeds; a render error of the screen is not swallowed; the loading
  line before the data does not load the chunk.
- `frontend/tests/static/brief-lazy.spec.js` (5): the blocked chunk at 390 and 1440 gives the failure box with a >= 44 px
  再読み込み, the stock still named, and the screen after the reload; a phone open after the idle-time prefetch, before the
  chunk has landed (the tap waits; one history entry), and with the chunk failing shows no loading frame and no stock address
  without its content.
  `6495f969` fixes this spec's own precondition for the after-idle case: it waited with `page.waitForFunction` on a predicate
  that returns a promise, which Playwright took as truthy at once (実測: it returned in 21 ms while the chunk was held). It now
  polls `page.evaluate`, which awaits the import: 実測, it times out (3 s) while the chunk is held and passes once the shell's
  loader holds the module. With it, a plain `lazy(() => import(...))` (`open()` still waiting) fails that case with
  「loading line at #/brief/stock/ZZNV」; before the fix the case was caught only through `open()`.
- `frontend/tools/entry-size.test.mjs` (optional item): the entry chunk of `frontend/dist` <= 134.00 kB gzip when a build is
  there, skipped otherwise. CI's test step runs before any build, so it does not guard CI; wiring a build-then-check step into
  `ci.yml` is proposed, not done (未検証).

Design Acceptance's four timing metrics with its own selectors and method (CDP CPU 4x, dark theme, the 11-12 rebuild's data, the
production build of each tree's HEAD), 7 runs per tree, the two trees interleaved, each page parked after its run. Each run first
waited for a quiet machine (1-minute load < 0.5, the harness's `wait-quiet.sh 0.5 600`); the load is recorded before and after
(the run's own Chrome raises it). Each variant was committed and built before it was measured. Medians (ms):

| Run (start JST) | load before → after | width | tree | first candidate | longest initial task | tab switch | stock open |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| band: main vs main (13:22) | 0.41 → 3.85 | 390 | main | 1996 | 84 | 223 | 435 |
| | | 390 | main | 2017 | 76 | 227 | 431 |
| | | 1440 | main | 2390 | 240 | 423 | 432 |
| | | 1440 | main | 2474 | 215 | 338 | 444 |
| main vs A (13:26) | 0.45 → 3.74 | 390 | main | 2002 | 78 | 208 | 461 |
| | | 390 | **A** | 2052 | 80 | 220 | 456 |
| | | 1440 | main | 2384 | 237 | 414 | 466 |
| | | 1440 | **A** | 2403 | **227** | **378** | 424 |
| main vs B (13:31) | 0.47 → 2.36 | 390 | main | 2051 | 81 | 270 | 505 |
| | | 390 | **B** | 2051 | 83 | 220 | 476 |
| | | 1440 | main | 2711 | 249 | 391 | 465 |
| | | 1440 | **B** | 2249 | **116** | **571** | 449 |

- Noise band (the larger of the two main medians' difference and 10 ms): 1440 longest initial task |240 − 215| = **25 ms**,
  tab switch |423 − 338| = **85 ms**; 390 longest 8 → **10 ms**, tab 4 → **10 ms**.
- **A** (the stock chunk starts at module start at >= 1024 px; the pane renders under `Suspense`, its placeholder being the
  pane's header and loading line): 1440 longest 227 <= 237 + 25 and tab 378 <= 414 + 85. **Qualifies.**
- **B** (A, plus: the first wide pane of a page shows its header and loading line with the first rows and mounts its body in
  `startTransition` once those rows have committed; commit `2f5fe43`, never pushed): 1440 longest 116 <= 249 + 25, but tab
  571 > 391 + 85. **Fails** the tab rule. Per run B is bimodal: where its deferred pane landed in the tab-switch window, the
  longest task read 58-116 ms and the tab switch 571-1055 ms; elsewhere 243-266 ms and 339-344 ms. The deferral moves the
  pane's work into the tab switch rather than removing it.
- Kept: **A**, the only variant that qualifies. B's commit was reset off the branch (unpushed); its numbers stay here.
- 390 (the phone code of A and B is the same): the tab switch read 220 against main's 208 in the A run (+12, band 10) and 220
  against 270 in the B run (−50); the longest task 80 / 78 and 83 / 81.

Two confirmation runs on the kept head, the same tool and rule, after every identity check (16:09 and 16:14 JST; load 0.39 →
3.05 and 0.46 → 2.59), medians (ms):

| Run | width | tree | first candidate | longest initial task | tab switch | stock open |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

#### The failure box on the production build (実測 by the S1-P1b verification; moved here from "Not measured here")

The S1-P1b verification ran a production-build probe on `693e70c` (S1-P1's seam with S1-P1b's 400 ms bound): the harness's
production build served from the local disk, the hashed `StockView` chunk's request held from its prefetch, 390x844, CPU 4x,
the CI browser, a fresh page load per run, 5 runs per mode, 0 page errors (load 0.82 → 1.59; a behaviour reading, not a timing
against main). With the held chunk aborted after the tap, the stock opened on its loading line and then on the failure box with
its message 「銘柄の画面を読み込めません（通信状態を確認して、再読み込みしてください）」 and 再読み込み, in 5 of 5 runs: the
production build's preload wrapper hands the failed import to the screen's failure box as the dev server does. The numbers are
in S1-P1b section 2. Not run on the production build: a chunk that fails at once (no hold), and a later tap after a failure.

#### Not measured here (未検証)

- Design Acceptance itself (28 screens, 86 checks) runs on the PR in CI; here only its selectors, chunk pattern and timing method
  were checked against the code, and its timing method reproduced by the yardstick.
- The effect of a real network on the cold phone open (the yardstick serves the build from the local disk).
- CI's own Playwright browser on the two pre-existing race failures above; and the full static suite on main today (only those
  two tests were run on main).
- The failure box on the production build for a chunk that fails at once (no hold) and for a later tap after a failure (the
  held-then-aborted case was measured by the S1-P1b verification; see above).
- Whether the 390 first-candidate drift and the one 1440 longest-task reading above the band (confirm 2) are effects of this
  package or of the host: open readings, not settled by three runs.
- Two behaviours of this package's seam under a stalled or recovered network (added in S1-P1b, from the S1-P1 verification):
  (1) on a phone, a tap while the chunk's request neither resolves nor rejects (a stalled connection) gave no visible response
  until the request settled, where main navigated at once; S1-P1b bounds this wait at 400 ms (with the bound, the S1-P1b
  verification measured the stalled tap on the production build: see S1-P1b section 2). (2) After a failed load, a later
  tap with the chunk reachable again still shows the failure box in this browser, because the browser keeps the failed module
  import for the page's life; the 再読み込み button (a page reload) is the only recovery (the S1-P1 verification's reading on
  the dev server; not run on the production build). Neither is measured on a real network or a real phone.

## S1-P1b The seam's three open orders (phone prefetch, stalled tap, record corrections)

Status: done. Started from `9f6a5d4e`. Code: `ba97db85` (variant: the phone's prefetch of the stock chunk after the first
rows), `3ca2bac5` (its revert: the variant failed its keep rule), `46aac2b7` (a phone tap that waits too long opens the
stock on its loading line), `8fa61646` (the spec: the held chunk released at the tap, and a stall that then fails). The record
commits change docs only. Verified (pass) on `693e70c`; its two round 1 orders are record-only (section 4). Nothing visible
changes in any yardstick view, so the allow-list stays empty (see "Allow-list" below).

### 1. The phone's prefetch of the stock chunk after the first rows: measured, failed its rule, reverted

What `ba97db85` did: on a phone, the idle-time prefetch of the stock chunk no longer ran in the first idle callback with the
market tab's chunk; an effect on the loaded bundle scheduled it at the next idle time after the render that commits the first
rows. The market tab's prefetch and the module-start load (the stock pane at 1024px and wider, a stock's own address) were
unchanged.

Keep rule (the order's, with its open terms written down before any reading of the variant): keep the variant if and only if
(a) its 1440 longest initial task and tab switch medians are <= main + band, (b) its 390 warm and cold stock open medians are
<= the S1-P1 head's + band, and (c) its 390 first-candidate median is <= the S1-P1 head's. Terms fixed in advance:
- Four sessions interleaved in one run (the tool's own rotation): A main `01066eb`, B main again, C the S1-P1 head `9f6a5d4`,
  D the variant `ba97db8`. The order asked for three trees; main ran twice so that the band comes from the same run.
- Band per metric and width = max(|A − B|, 10 ms), from the pooled values.
- Warm: the yardstick's `timing4` spec (Design Acceptance's four metrics, CPU 4x, dark), 7 runs per run, three runs, pooled
  to 21 per session. Cold: a copy of S1-P1's cold-open spec (the first row clicked as soon as it is visible), 390 only, also
  three runs of 7, pooled to 21. Each run waited for a quiet machine (1-minute load < 0.5); the top processes recorded
  before each run show no foreign process at 5% CPU or more.
- The verdict reads only (a), (b), (c); the slow-mode share of the first candidate (> 2400 ms) is information only. No run
  was added after a result was seen.

Verdict: three of the five conditions fail, so the variant was reverted with `git revert` (`3ca2bac5`); the tree is again
byte-identical to `9f6a5d4` (`git diff 9f6a5d4e 3ca2bac5` is empty), and the phone prefetches the stock chunk in the first idle
callback as in S1-P1. How firm the verdict is: (a)'s failure depends on which main session is the reference (against B,
375 would pass 354 + 71), but the revert does not rest on (a): (b)'s warm stock open and (c) compare the variant with the
S1-P1 head only, and the warm stock open fails S1-P1's recorded 10 ms band as well. Readings that go with the verdict
(information, not part of the rule):
- In these runs every session was in the slower first-candidate mode: > 2400 ms in 21 of 21 runs for A, B and C and 20 of 21
  for D. Main has no stock chunk and was in it as often, so in these runs the slow mode does not depend on the stock chunk's
  prefetch; the S1-P1 reading (S1 slow 18/35, main 12/35) cannot be settled by these runs, and its cause stays open.
- At 1440 C and D run the same code (the chunk starts at module start there), yet their longest-task medians differ by 30 ms,
  and the two main sessions by 71 ms: at 1440 the scatter of one session's 21-run median is of that size on this host.
- The cold open is not a tap that waits for the chunk: a request log of one cold open on the S1-P1 head build (390, CPU 4x)
  shows the stock chunk requested in the first idle callback while the data loads (868 ms after navigation) and loaded at
  1191 ms, before the first row was visible (2701 ms). In the three cold runs the time from the click to the history push of
  the stock address was 34, 38 and 37 ms for C (run medians; pooled 34, max 61) against 28-38 ms for main, which never waits
  (pooled 32, max 47): the click found the chunk loaded. See the correction in S1-P1's "Cold stock open".

### 2. A tap on a stalled connection opens the stock on its loading line

Before (S1-P1): on a phone, a row tap whose stock chunk was not loaded navigated only when `loadStockView()` settled. On a
connection that neither delivers nor fails the chunk, the tap gave no visible response.

Now (`46aac2b7`): the tap still waits for the chunk, but at most `STOCK_TAP_WAIT_MS` = **400 ms** (`stockViewLoader.js`).
If the chunk has not settled by then, `open()` navigates anyway and the stock opens on the screen's existing loading line
(「銘柄データを読み込み中…」, the `Suspense` fallback; the bar names the stock), which gives way to the screen when the chunk
arrives, or to the failure box with its 再読み込み when it fails. The timer and the chunk's settling share one guard:
whichever comes first navigates, once (one history entry); a newer tap cancels the older one's timer, so only the last tap
opens; and nothing opens if the screen changed meanwhile. At >= 1024 px nothing changes (`open()` never waits there).

Why 400 ms:
- 実測, the wait for a chunk that is merely on its way: a probe on the S1-P1 head build (390, CPU 4x, the local server)
  that holds the stock chunk's request until the tap and releases it at the tap measured tap → navigation 54-111 ms
  (7 runs: 111, 62, 55, 71, 72, 57, 54; load 0.44 → 1.33). 400 ms leaves such a tap opening the whole screen.
- With Lighthouse's mobile network applied at the tap (150 ms round trip, 1.64 Mbps down) the same probe read 534-569 ms,
  but the local server sends the chunk uncompressed (68.9 kB); the published site sends it gzip-compressed (24.46 kB), which
  at that bandwidth is about 0.12 s of transfer plus the round trip and the run, about 0.3 s (computed, 未検証 on a real
  phone). A tap there opens the whole screen; on a slower or stalled network it opens on the loading line at 400 ms.
- 400 ms keeps a stalled tap's visible response within about the time people still read as an immediate answer; a shorter
  bound would make more ordinary cold taps open on a loading frame first.
- In normal use the bound is rarely reached: the chunk is prefetched while the data loads (section 1), so a tap finds it
  loaded.

Deliberate test change (`brief-lazy.spec.js`, "before the chunk has landed: the tap waits for it, then opens the whole
screen"):
- Old: the chunk held for 600 ms after the tap, then the URL still `#/brief`, then release, the whole screen, no loading
  frame, back to Today.
- New (`46aac2b7`, then `8fa61646`): the chunk released right after the tap (well inside the bound); then the whole screen;
  no empty stock frame and no loading line before the bound; back to Today. `8fa61646` dropped the two checks between the
  tap and the release (the address still `#/brief`, Today's first row visible): they ran while the 400 ms timer did and could
  fail on a slow runner; a tap that navigates at once is still caught by the no-loading-line-before-the-bound check.
- Why: with the bound, a 600 ms hold now opens the loading line at 400 ms by design (that case is the new stalled test).
  On the dev server the released chunk settled inside the bound in three diagnostic runs (no loading frame at all), so the
  case still exercises the wait.

New tests (`brief-lazy.spec.js`, 8 in all now):
- "on a stalled connection: the tap opens the stock on its loading line at the bound, then the screen": the chunk held; the
  stock address and the loading line after the tap, the bar naming the stock; the loading line no earlier than the bound and
  less than the bound + 300 ms after the tap (the slack is the page transition's capture frame on a loaded CI machine); after
  release the screen and the chart, no empty stock frame; one 戻る returns to Today (one history entry).
  The ceiling (bound + 300 ms) is a wall-clock assert. It held in 10 of 10 CI-faithful repeats in the S1-P1b verification, and
  on the production build at CPU 4x the loading line came 438-534 ms after the tap (below); a loaded CI runner is not
  measured. Standing rule (the verification's order, no change now): if this assert fails in CI, re-run once; if it fails
  again, widen the slack (for example bound + 600 ms) as a deliberate test change listed in this record. The lower bound
  (no earlier than the bound − 2 ms) and the no-loading-line-before-the-bound checks stay: they catch a tap that navigates
  at once.
- "on a stalled connection: of two taps only the last opens, once": two taps on different rows while the chunk is held; the
  second row's stock opens on its loading line, then its screen; no frame of the first row's stock; one 戻る returns to Today.
- "on a stalled connection that then fails: the loading line gives way to the failure box" (`8fa61646`): the chunk held past
  the bound, then aborted; the loading line, then the failure box with its message, the bar still naming the stock; no empty
  stock frame and no loading line before the bound.
- 実測, the tests fail on the old behaviours (run on `46aac2b7` before `8fa61646`): with the timer removed (S1-P1's
  behaviour) the two stalled tests fail; with `open()` navigating at once (main's behaviour) the changed test and the two
  stalled tests fail.
- 実測, stability: the phone cases (6 tests) repeated 10 times each (`--repeat-each 10`, with a temporary print): 60 passed;
  the loading line appeared 422-438 ms after the tap (bound 400, ceiling 700).

Known behaviour on the production build (実測 by the S1-P1b verification on `693e70c`; not re-run by this package): a probe
on the harness's production build served from the local disk, the hashed `StockView` chunk's request held from its prefetch,
390x844, CPU 4x, the CI browser, a fresh page load per run, 5 runs per mode; in every run of every mode one history push of
the stock address and history +1, one 戻る returned to Today, and no page error. Load 0.82 → 1.59, not interleaved against
main: a behaviour reading, not a timing comparison. Times from the tap (ms):

| Mode | history push | loading line | then |
|---|---|---|---|
| held, released after the loading line (a stalled connection that recovers) | 422-449 | 450-534 | the screen |
| held, aborted after the loading line (a stalled connection that fails) | 417-451 | 438-528 | the failure box 「銘柄の画面を読み込めません（通信状態を確認して、再読み込みしてください）」 with 再読み込み, 5 of 5 |
| released at the tap (a chunk on its way) | 49-72 | none (no loading frame) | the screen |

So on a stalled connection a tap answers at the bound with the stock's loading line, and the screen or, if the chunk then
fails, the failure box follows; the stalled-then-failed case is no longer measured on the dev server only.

Still 未検証:
- A real network and a real phone (every reading above serves the build from the local disk).
- On the production build: a chunk that fails at once (no hold), and the two-tap case (both run on the dev server only, in
  `brief-lazy.spec.js`).
- After a failed load of the chunk the browser keeps the failed module import for the page's life: a later tap, even with
  the chunk reachable again, opens the failure box again at once. The 44 px 再読み込み (a page reload) is the recovery.
  (The S1-P1 verification's reading on the dev server; not re-run in S1-P1b, and not run on the production build, where
  each probe run loaded the page afresh.)

## S1-P2 Market gate model (pure functions; nothing renders yet)

Status: done. Started from `42f8aa05`. Code: `5363d154` (`marketLines`), `ec5867cc` (the D12 move into
`marketGateFacts.js`), `e096f8cd` (a lint fix of `5363d154`'s test: the repository's lint rule forbids a message argument to
`expect`), `2b0af277` (`marketGate.js`, its tests, the reader option of `marketCardView`). The record commits change docs only.
The verification passed it with three minor orders; its round 1 fix is `eff919cc` (test only; subsection below). Its round 2
(on `70468d84`) passed it again with two minor orders, both record only: no code change (last subsection below).
No component imports the new model yet, so nothing visible changes: every yardstick view, the ledger, the probe, the dump and
the surfaces are identical to main's, and the allow-list stays empty.

### What changed

- `frontend/src/static/portfolioPlan.js`: new export `marketLines(rows)` → `{ above50, above200 }`, read with `modelMarket`'s
  own filters (US rows in USD with a regime and both lines as booleans; a line is false when any of those rows has it false,
  as `modelMarket`'s below50 / below200). Both lines are null when no row passes. `modelMarket` and `portfolioPlan.test.js`
  are byte-identical to main's.
- `frontend/src/static/brief/marketGateFacts.js` (new, D12): the readers the gate needs that sat in the lazily loaded market
  modules, moved verbatim or taken out whole:
  - `publishedMarketContext` (with its two private checks) and `REGIME_NAMES`, from `reference/marketTabStudy.js`, which
    imports them back and re-exports them under the same names;
  - `breakoutView` (the breakout row), from `marketTabOnly.js`, which re-exports it;
  - `leaderHealthView` (the leader-health row and the cohort its rate reads), taken out of `marketTabParts` in
    `marketTabGroups.js`; the market tab passes its RS推計90 line (`rs90CohortLine`) as the row's opened lines, as before;
  - `stockSideTerms` (the 銘柄側 line's three terms and its shared date), taken out of `stockSideLine`, which still words the line;
    `STOCK_SIDE_HEAD` moves with it and is re-exported by `marketTabGroups.js`;
  - `gateReads(shared)`: what the gate reads of the 地合い card's shared values (the breadth rows, TT達成, the two indices'
    counts, the new highs and lows, the breakout and leader-health rows with the raw counts their rates read, the 銘柄側 terms).
  Every export the market tab's modules had keeps its name and module, and their tests are unchanged and green.
- `frontend/src/static/brief/marketTabModel.js`: `marketCardView` takes an optional `facts` reader (as the market tab passes
  `tab`) and returns its result as `gate`; without it `gate` is null and nothing more is computed. The shared values are built
  once and handed to both readers.
- `frontend/src/static/brief/marketGate.js` (new):
  - `marketBand(market, { loading, late, suffix, date, target })`: `state` (loading, unknown, waiting, open, closed),
    `subject`, `answer`, `label`, `guide` (`capText`), `guideLabel`, `kinds` (line 2's labels, a long and a short form),
    `line1`, `line1Labelled` (the labels inline, the late form of the ladder), `chip`, `name` (the full sentence with the
    regime's label), `tone` (`marketTone`, for the call card's own tone; the band itself is text ink, D17), `age` (only a
    stale publication's age), `sign` (the tile's word) and `waiting`.
  - `resumeConditions(market, lines)`: the 200-day line, the 50-day line and the regime in `modelMarket`'s rule order, each
    met / unmet / unknown; `title` 「買いの再開の条件」 or 「目安が動く条件」; `first` (the unmet and unknown ones by name, the two
    lines named together) and `fold` (the met ones); 「再開の条件：未確認」 when nothing is known. No count is printed.
  - `gateSign(...)`: the sign line from the 地合い tile's own reading (FTD, 反発n日目, NAS調整中) and the withheld form of a
    series one session behind.
  - `marketGate(...)`: the gate card's head (kicker, headline with 独自判定, the wide headline, the figure with its label and
    its definition), its basis line (`verdictBasis` with 実測 and the conflict note), conditions, sign line, fold summary and
    `todayNote`.
  - `gateFacts(brief, { lines, target, view })`: the facts `{ key, group, label, value, reading, kind, asOf }` in the four
    groups, each kind fixed by `FACT_KINDS`.
  - Constants: `BAND_SUBJECT`, `GATE_KINDS` (the seven kinds), `NO_KIND` 「—」, `GATE_GROUPS`, `FACT_KINDS`, `CONDITION_KIND`,
    `SIGN_KIND`, `SIGN_TITLE`, `CONDITION_LABELS` (from `REGIME_NAMES` and `MARKET_CAPS`) and `REGIME_DISTRIBUTION_PRESSURE` = 4
    (the backend's `DIST_UNDER_PRESSURE`, read by a test).
  - Nothing reads the clock: `target` (the last session the exchange calendar says has closed, `marketUpdateState().target`,
    or null) is a required argument of `marketBand`, `marketGate` and `gateFacts` (a TypeError when it is left out), and the
    waiting form is `verdictWaiting`'s own.

### Decisions and deviations (each for the conductor to confirm or overrule)

1. **Signatures.** `marketBand` takes `date` and `target` besides the plan's `{ loading, late, suffix }` (D18 needs both);
   `resumeConditions` takes `lines` (`modelMarket`'s return carries no line state and stays pinned); `gateFacts` takes
   `useBriefData`'s shape plus `{ lines, target, view }`. The plan names "the gate" without a function; it is `marketGate`
   (and `gateSign`), because the state table's head, conditions, sign and summary columns need one model.
2. **The D12 move set** is larger than the plan's three names: the stock-side figures need the breakout row (`breakoutView`,
   in `marketTabOnly.js`, a file the plan does not list) and the leader-health row (inline in `marketTabParts`). Both moved;
   the market tab builds its rows from them, with the same values (its tests are unchanged).
3. **SPY 分配日's kind** is 「実測・基準は独自設定」: S3-P2's table writes 「実測（基準と段階は独自設定）」, which is not one of the
   seven kinds S1-P2 lists. Its display grade (参考（5〜6日）/ 注意（7日以上）) is a separate field, `tier`.
4. **The breakout row's name** is the app's one constant `BREAKOUT_HOLD_NAME` 「上抜け維持（確定分）」, the market tab's name.
   The plan writes 上抜けの維持; printing that on Today beside the market tab's name would give one row two names.
5. **Absent words.** A fact without a value prints its row's own words (読み込み中…, 取得できません, 未配信, 未算出 …; 未収録 for
   the regime's SPY count, as `publishedMarketContext`'s rule says), never the plan's generic 「未取得」, and its kind is 「—」.
   The regime's block is read only when its regime is the verdict's rows' (`regimeCause`'s rule).
6. **An unknown market's conditions are all unknown**, even when rows of two regimes carry observed lines (the table's
   "mixed rows" row: never 未達 or 達成). Under `modelMarket`'s filter a judged market always has both lines, so the table's
   "part of the conditions unknown" row cannot be reached from rows: the yardstick's `partunk` recipe (every 50-day field
   missing) gives 市場未確認 and 「再開の条件：未確認」, not the per-line form its comment expected. The per-condition form is
   pinned with a hand-built `lines` input. Reading the lines without the filter would be a second gate (D14 would fail for
   rows that each carry only one line).
7. **The FTD sign line** names an FTD older than 25 sessions by the existing rule: 「前回のFTD 8/13（参考・目安は 0%のまま：
   SPYが50日線の下）」; a recent one 「FTD確認済み（参考・目安は 0%のまま：SPYが50日線の下）」.
8. **The withheld sign** (「未確認（10/2分は再集計待ち）」) is given only when an index record is older than the analysis date;
   a series that is not ready gives no sign line (`StatusAlert` speaks for it).

- `frontend/src/static/brief/marketGate.test.js` (new, 25 tests, node environment; the states are opened from the frozen
  browser fixture with the specs' own mutators, copied with their source lines):

    failure and two regimes), part of the conditions unknown, loading, one session behind (`todayNote`, the withheld sign),

    10/10, 2026-11-19, Tue 10/6 16:00Z, Wed 10/7 16:00Z, `TWO_LATER` and `WITHIN_GRACE`) × 50% / 25% / 0%: `target` is
    `marketUpdateState(asset, now).target`, and the band's waiting form equals the 市場 tab's `verdict.leadShort` (and the wide
    headline its `lead`) in every case; 「検討可」 / 「検討できる」 never appear while waiting in the band, the gate head and
    (since `eff919cc`; before it the facts were not checked) `gateFacts`, whose verdict reads 「<label>・10/2時点の判定・
    更新待ち」 with the guide dated 10/2; the late publication (age 「2営業日前」 from `freshnessLine` at `TWO_LATER`) changes
    only `age`, never the answer, at 50%, 25% and 0%;
  - no clock: seven states give byte-identical output under two system times; leaving `target` out throws;
  - D14: all 3 × 3 × 7 inputs (each line true / false / missing × five regimes, none, two regimes), read from rows through
    `modelMarket` and `marketLines`: all conditions met exactly when the cap is above 0; an unknown market's conditions all
    unknown, a judged market's all known; two regimes never a met regime; an unrecognised regime never printed by its key;
  - the band's line, chip and name from one value in 21 states (the same subject, figure and labels; every figure preceded by
    目安; no bare 独自; no ellipsis); no 「条件中」, no ASCII minus before a digit, no 0 for a missing fact, no order words;
  - the kind table pinned, each fact in exactly one group, the values of a fully published day, the SPY count's grades and
    its 未収録 forms, the backend threshold, and the small-sample and missing-count forms of the breakout and health rows.
- Sensitivity (実測, by hand, reverted): with the regime condition always met, 5 tests fail; with a figure in the unknown form,
  3; with the waiting form ignored, 2; with an unknown market's regime read, 2.

## S1-P3 The band in the bar: stopped at step 0 (the fit proof)

Status: **stopped at step 0**, before any component was written. Started from `42cc10cb`. The plan's kind-label ladder for the
phone bar ((a) → (b) → (c) → stop) reaches its stop rung for three states from two rows of the state table (the pending and
the late forms of an open guide, and the late publication at 0%), and for the calendar-less bar, which the table does not list,
at one or more widths (below). As the
plan and the order require, no truncated or partial band ships: no component, no CSS, no spec and no test was written or
changed, so there is no code commit and nothing to revert. The package's only commits are this section's record commits. Owner lines,
caps, budgets, tokens, the waiver, the fixture and the frozen specs are untouched. The verification confirmed the stop on
`78d602b8` ("The verification's verdict" below); the branch's code is S1-P2's.

### How the fit was measured (実測, 2026-11-19, a scratch Playwright spec on this worktree's dev server at `42cc10cb`)

- **The real bar.** The phone 候補 screen of this worktree (its own dev server on port 4461, Chrome for Testing 147 headless
  shell, the CI fonts, the fixture's `openBrief` at its pinned clock, reduced motion), at 320x640, 375x812 and 390x844. The
  bar's title element (`.brief-bar-title`, with the theme toggle beside it as on main) is given each candidate form: line 1 as
  its `b` (16 px bold by the bar's own rule; rung (c) at 14 px bold), line 2 as the bar's own date line
  (`span.numeric.brief-bar-date > span.brief-bar-age` holding the `<time datetime>`), so every rule of `brief.css` and
  `brief-watch.css` that styles the bar applies (line 2 at 12 px below 521 px, in the mono stack as `BarDate`).
- **The numbers.** For each line: `scrollWidth` and `clientWidth`, and the content width read from a hidden max-content clone
  beside the line (same parent, same selectors and fonts). The slack is `clientWidth − content width`: `scrollWidth` never
  drops below `clientWidth`, so it cannot show slack. A form is usable only with **>= 8 px of slack on both lines**, at the
  fixture's date 10/2（金） and at the widest dates 12/28 and 11/30 (two-digit month and day). The worst date is given beside
  the fixture date.
- **The strings are the model's.** The same run imports `marketGate.js` and `portfolioPlan.js` into the page through the dev
  server (the first time `marketGate.js` has executed in a browser) and checks `marketBand()`'s `line1`, `line1Labelled` and
  `kinds` for the fixture's rows (上昇トレンド), the 上昇に圧力 and 50日線割れ recipes, the pending and late forms (target 10/5
  and 10/6, the late age 「2営業日前」) and the unknown (the `partunk` recipe and a missing manifest) and loading forms against
  the strings measured: all equal.
- **A first run was wrong and is not used.** It measured the clone outside the app's root, where neither the bar's font rules
  nor the theme's font variables apply. The corrected widths reproduce the critic's isolated-span widths (216.9 / 224.0 /
  208.8 px here against 216.9 / 224.0 / 209.1).
- **Line widths available** (the title's `clientWidth`): **248 / 303 / 318 px** at 320 / 375 / 390. The bar is 48 px in
  every measured form.

### Before Today's first screen changed: the prefetch decision

S1-P1b's keep rule result is in this record (S1-P1b, section 1): the phone's deferred prefetch of the stock chunk failed its rule
and was reverted (`3ca2bac5`); the phone prefetches the stock chunk in the first idle callback, as in S1-P1. That is the state
this package started from (`stockViewLoader.js` and the prefetch effect in `BriefApp.jsx` are unchanged by this package).

  「地合い」). It builds `marketCardView` with `update: { target }` and the gate's reader (`gateReads`), `marketGate` and `gateFacts`
  once. At 1024 px and wider it keeps the step header, the verdict block (`data-testid="brief-regime"`) and the index chart; the
  verdict block prints the waiting lead (`verdict.lead`, D18) in place of its text while a newer session has closed under an open
  guide, and its text otherwise (byte-identical to main). Below 1024 px neither is drawn (main hid both below 521 px and showed
  them at 521-1023 px; the gate's headline is the answer there now). `Fact` takes an optional kind tag. The IBD line stays last,
  outside the fold.

    warning tone on a late publication) with 「市場の詳細 ›」 (44 px); the headline (20 px bold) with 「（独自判定）」 (12 px; none in

fix, both inside the closed fold or without a date) reads every phone state identically.

### Decisions and deviations (each for the conductor to confirm or overrule)

1. **The head's layout.** The figure's label sits left of the figure on one line under the headline, not over the figure at the
   headline's right: the plan's form made the waiting state taller and broke the equality pin (above). Every ★ line is present.
2. **The regime's label** is in the fold's 判定 row, not in the first fold: the first fold's headline is the answer with 独自判定;
   printing the label there ( 「新規の買いを検討できる（上昇トレンド・独自判定）」) wraps the headline to a second line (+27 px) and the
   governing state has 2 px of room.
3. **`target` is computed in `BriefApp.jsx`**, not in `TodayView.jsx`, so it is re-read at each close with the bar's age (the 市場
   tab reads `marketUpdateState()` with the clock at render, as before). `BriefApp.jsx` is not in the plan's file list.
4. **521-1023 px** follow the phone (decided on `wide`, 1024 px): the gate card with its fold closed, no verdict block and no index
   chart (main showed both there). No yardstick view and no Today spec uses those widths (`grep` of `tests/static` for viewports
   from 521 to 1023: `brief-compact.spec.js:329` at 768 reads 候補 and `brief-market.spec.js:372` at 900 reads 市場).
5. **A firing ▲ cannot be reached in the browser on this data:** every rule Today reads (`environmentRules`: template, stage,
   leaders, breakout; and the split) is in `REFERENCE_CHECKS`, so on Today each reads ○ 参考 and sits in the fold. The ▲ lines'
   first-fold place is pinned by a unit test with a synthetic view (`TodayGate.test.jsx`); `brief-gate.spec.js` asserts that no ○
   line is in the closed first fold.
6. **The withheld sign** reads 「サイン：未確認（10/2分は再集計待ち）」 (the model's 「未確認（…）」 alone did not say what is unconfirmed).
7. **The FTD conflict's cause line is printed** in the fold (the state table's 「下に詳細」), so S1-P2's conditional task was done:
   `regimeCause` and `gspcFtdWords` moved into `marketGateFacts.js` in their own commit (`2a6287a7`), re-exported under their names,
   their tests unchanged; `TodayGate.test.jsx` pins the ftdconflict row's first-fold sentence (the 根拠 line with
   「（指数の履歴と不一致・下に詳細）」) and its cause line 「局面判定はSPYのFTD（9/23）による上昇トレンド。^GSPCの日足ではFTD未確認。この目安は
   SPYが50日線の下のため0%（独自判定）」 in the fold only. The move's entry delta (実測, a scratch build of this head with the card's
   cause import removed): 112.41 kB gzip without it, 112.47 with it: **+0.06 kB gzip** (+0.13 raw). The line is printed only in
   the basis or FTD conflict.
8. **Kind tags on Today's four facts** are appended inside each `Fact` (12 px), so the facts' label-value pairs change in the
   values tool (allow-listed below); their lines and numbers do not.
9. **The 1440 surfaces.** The cross-surface oracle gained two rows (`today-gate@390`, `today-gate@1440`) in this package; see
   "Checks".

### Allow-list (cumulative for S1)

(Regenerated in round 1, the end of this section: 710 entries, 4 + 706, sha256 `9751ddbd…a9511`; this paragraph's counts are
S1-P4's first list.) S1-P1 to S1-P3 added nothing. S1-P4 adds 735 entries: 4 for the cross-surface oracle and 731 for the values. A values entry is
one difference of the diff above: its kind, **one section**, its exact text and the exact view ids it changed in for that section
(an anchored alternation; no wildcard pattern), so an entry never allows a text in a section or a view it did not change in. The
machine-readable copy is the yardstick's scratch allow-list file (sha256 `afd34440…d3cc`, 735 entries: `tool` `surfaces` or
`values`, each with its reason and `package` `S1-P4`); both tools read it (each skips the other's entries). 実測: with it, the
values diff gives 19756 differences and 0 failures, and the surfaces diff 65 and 0. It still catches a leak: a copy of this
head's values in which the fold's 未検証 line is put into `375 m50/today`'s closed lines fails (1 FAIL, `added closed.lines`;
a first build of the list keyed on the text alone, with every section and view of a text in one entry, would have allowed it and
was replaced before this record).

### The verification's round 1 orders (verdict fix)

Started from `3b9e6c06` (tree clean). Code: `3ff04cc7` (order 1: the waiting headline), `df67b190` (order 2: ledger step 3,
the short 根拠 form), `054f89f2` (order 3: the answers' label and value apart), `432d2b17` (order 4: 判定 once), `b10c8c91`
(order 6 (c): the clock handed to `marketUpdateState`). Tests: `c2161996` (the new pins in `brief-gate.spec.js`), `79772ca7`
and `401e9d3f` (the re-homed readings). The record commits change docs only. No ★ line is shortened, merged, folded or dropped;
no cap, budget, token, waiver, build target, Design Acceptance tool, fixture or frozen spec was edited.

The measuring spec of this round (scratch, CI fonts, Chrome for Testing 147, reduced motion, the worktree's dev server; main on
port 4462) reads Today's stack bottom (the specs' `contentHeight`), the first 注目候補 row's bottom and the dock's top, and
replaces a two-digit date in place where named: **every** M/D the card prints (kicker, headline, figure label, sign, note)
becomes 12/28 or 11/19 and a late age becomes two digits (「・12営業日前」). (The verification's spec replaced only the dates
followed by （ or 時点, which leaves the age; this one covers both.)

#### Order 1: the waiting form is exactly as tall as the open form

- **Cause (実測, the text widths in the card's own fonts).** The headline's line is 313 px at 375 (328 at 390, 266 at 320).
  「12/28時点の判定・更新待ち」 at 20 px bold is 250.1 px (every two-digit date measured the same: 12/28, 11/19, 10/30, 12/31,
  10/28; 「10/2」 238.9), 「（独自判定）」 at 12 px 72 px: 322.1 > 313, so the tag wrapped to a second 27 px line. The open
  headline is 220 + 72 = 292.
- **Ledger step 2 does not fit** (measured, not taken): the kicker is 131.4-138.0 px (211.4 with 「・12営業日前」) and the shortest
  headline with its tag (「買いは控える（独自判定）」) 192 px: 323 > 313 at 375 in every state with an analysis date (without one,
  the manifest failure, the kicker is 「地合い」 alone and the headline 「買いは見送り」 has no tag), so the kicker cannot share the
  headline's row. Step 3 does not touch the headline. Proportional punctuation (`palt` / `halt`) changes nothing in the CI fonts
  (the same widths, measured).
- **The fix (a layout change; no ★ line shortened, merged, folded or dropped; 独自判定 stays beside the headline).** In the waiting
  form only, the headline's kind is an inline block set in two short lines beside the headline, 「（独自」「判定）」 (one `<wbr>`,
  `word-break: keep-all`, 36 px wide, line height 1.1: 2 x 13.2 px inside the headline's own 27 px line). 250.1 + 36 = 286.1 px:
  **26.9 px of slack at 375** (41.9 at 390). The text is unchanged (「（独自判定）」 is one string in innerText and in the
  values; an inline block adds no line break). The open, closed and unknown forms keep the one-line tag. For the conductor's
  ruling: the tag's look changes in the waiting form only (two 12 px lines instead of one), its words and place do not.
- **Measured after (実測, 375 and 390; pending 10/5 20:10Z, late 10/7 09:00Z; native 10/2, 12/28 and 11/19, the late age
  「・12営業日前」 with them):**

#### Order 3: the two answers' label and value apart

A real space between the label (`small`) and the value (`b`) of each answer link; the label's right margin goes from 6 px to
2 px so the gap stays 6 px (5.9 measured): every tile's height and box are identical to `3b9e6c06`'s at 320 / 375 / 390 with no
watch list, four watched and five watched with one unlisted (実測). innerText 「注目候補 21銘柄」, 「ウォッチの注意 0件」; the
accessible names (実測, aria snapshot at 375): 「注目候補 21銘柄 ゾーン5・ 接近16 ・10/2（金）引け時点」 and 「ウォッチの注意 0件
4銘柄を確認・ 保有未登録 ・10/2（金）引け時点」 (the space after 「・」 is the existing `brief-frag` break). Asserted in
`brief-gate.spec.js` (the two answers at 320 / 375 / 390). The allow-list's `answers` entries are regenerated (below).
Limit, stated plainly: the values tool's `tile|` and `li|` pairs still carry label and value together in their label slot
(`li|注目候補 21銘柄|ゾーン5・接近16 / …`, now with the space), because the label and the value share one line; main's binding
(`li|注目候補|21銘柄 / …`) is not restored. The link names now equal main's.

#### Order 4: 判定 once

The fold's first group keeps `role="group"` with the name 判定 and has no visible heading: the row's own label says 判定 once
(the other three groups keep their headings). `TodayGate.test.jsx` asserts the group names, the three headings and the group's
text 「判定上昇トレンド・新規の買いを検討できる独自判定」; `brief-market-r3.spec.js` M1c reads the 判定 group by its name and asserts
it has no `h3`.
The values change accordingly (実測, `values-diff` on `401e9d3f`): the added line 「判定」 counts once per view where it counted
twice (×2 → ×1 in each of the 96 view-modes that show the fold: the 77 Today and stock views at 1440, in their closed and
opened lines, and the 19 Today views at 375, in their opened lines).
No allow-list entry changes for it, because an entry names a kind, a section, a text and its views, never a count.

#### Order 5: the harness dump
